I don't know, a few brief periods where I could function a little better. Like I said before... the brunches, the painting, hiking... it all just feels like work now. Even if I force myself to do it, it's just... going through the motions. There's no joy in it anymore.\n\nDoctor: Has anything significant happened in your life around the time when these feelings first started?\n\nPatient: Um... well, it started about two years and three months ago. I guess... I had just bought a townhouse around then. Just... feeling the pressure of trying to keep up with it all, I suppose. That's when it began.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
